### 1116. `RD_ALifeLine_41ed331b59c32b59` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Gotta put it down right now, it's a step away"
- **TR:** 'Hemen indirmem lazım, bir adım ötede'

### 1117. `RD_ALifeLine_50192c417f4ca73c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Watch your feet, you'll go over backward"
- **TR:** 'Ayağına dikkat et, sırtüstü düşeceksin'

### 1118. `RD_ALifeLine_575aa5a74bea7af2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Oh, that's close enough, that's way too close"
- **TR:** 'Ah, bu kadar yeter, bu çok fazla yakın'

### 1119. `RD_ALifeLine_59cb31417d8ae63e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Too close for comfort, buddy, way too close'
- **TR:** 'Fazla yakın, birader, çok fazla yakın'

### 1120. `RD_ALifeLine_2dd4525468924d74` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'OH- SHIT! ... SHIT.. SHIT .. SHiTTTTTT!!!'
- **TR:** 'HASSİKTİR! ... SİKTİR.. SİKTİR .. SİKTİRRRRR!!!'

### 1121. `RD_ALifeLine_0434a3ff36eecaca` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "It's RIGHT THERE! It's right there! Get it off, get it OFF!"
- **TR:** 'TAM ORADA! Tam orada! Çekin şunu, ÇEKİN!'

### 1122. `RD_ALifeLine_523dda543a776428` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'No no no no, too close, too close, TOO CLOSE!!'
- **TR:** 'Yok yok yok yok, çok yakın, çok yakın, ÇOK YAKIN!!'

### 1123. `RD_ALifeLine_4303f4f7414378ee` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Back- back- BACK UP!! It's reaching, it's REACHING!"
- **TR:** 'Geri- geri- GERİ ÇEKİL!! Uzanıyor, UZANIYOR!'

### 1124. `RD_ALifeLine_5f8960955480bcc8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Aaaah! Don't touch me! DON'T TOUCH ME!"
- **TR:** 'Aaaah! Bana dokunma! BANA DOKUNMA!'

### 1125. `RD_ALifeLine_54c276187415b236` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Ohhh God, the SMELL, it's right in my face-"
- **TR:** "Aman Allah'ım, şu KOKU, tam suratımda-"

### 1126. `RD_ALifeLine_28af0f7c4632f101` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Go away, go AWAY, goawaygoaway-'
- **TR:** 'Git, GİT, gitgitgit-'

### 1127. `RD_ALifeLine_09f87fd141a25998` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Mommy- mommy no, no, it's so close, it's so- it's so-"
- **TR:** 'Anne- anneciğim hayır, hayır, çok yakın, çok- çok-'

### 1128. `RD_ALifeLine_75843428057d9f93` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Get back! Get BACK! I'll- I don't know what I'll do, GET BACK!"
- **TR:** 'Geri çekil! GERİ! Ben- ne yapacağımı bilmiyorum, GERİ ÇEKİL!'

### 1129. `RD_ALifeLine_345c51bc481ee6cc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "It's got- it nearly- oh my God it nearly had my sleeve!"
- **TR:** "Neredeyse- az kalsın- aman Allah'ım az kalsın kolumu yakalıyordu!"

### 1130. `RD_ALifeLine_0eea579978f582e7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Heeelp! Somebody- ANYBODY- it's on me, it's right on me!!"
- **TR:** 'İmdaaat! Biri- HERHANGİ BİRİ- üstümde, tam üstümde!!'

### 1131. `RD_ALifeLine_2578e4341106aea0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Not me! Take him, take anybody, just not ME!'
- **TR:** 'Ben değil! Onu al, kimi istersen al, yeter ki BEN olmayayım!'

### 1132. `RD_ALifeLine_6d51f2500ad74562` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Ahh- AH- hands, its hands, get its hands off!'
- **TR:** 'Ahh- AH- elleri, elleri, çekin ellerini!'

### 1133. `RD_ALifeLine_525b43b16fb90d7c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "I can't move, I can't, my legs won't- come on legs, COME ON-"
- **TR:** 'Kıpırdayamıyorum, yapamıyorum, bacaklarım- hadi bacaklar, HADİ-'

### 1134. `RD_ALifeLine_59c813c66f0e122c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Noooo, nooo, please, please please plleease-'
- **TR:** 'Hayııır, hayııır, lütfen, lütfen lütfen lütfeeen-'

### 1135. `RD_ALifeLine_411284ee713cbc8a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "It's so close I can see its eyes, oh God, its EYES-"
- **TR:** "O kadar yakın ki gözlerini görüyorum, Allah'ım, GÖZLERİ-"

### 1136. `RD_ALifeLine_28c4f9f152371173` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Don't let it get me, don't let it, promise me, PROMISE-"
- **TR:** 'Beni yakalamasına izin verme, verme, söz ver, SÖZ VER-'

### 1137. `RD_ALifeLine_799e4ce15b656dd9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Run! RUN! No wait, I can't, I can't, which way?!"
- **TR:** 'Kaç! KAÇ! Yok dur, yapamam, yapamam, ne tarafa?!'

### 1138. `RD_ALifeLine_384314e01ca9c782` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Stop it! Stop! Why won't it stop?!"
- **TR:** 'Dur! Dursana! Neden durmuyor?!'

### 1139. `RD_ALifeLine_1efb101e24dd85c9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Shit shit shit shit, it's inches, it's INCHES away!"
- **TR:** 'Siktir siktir siktir siktir, burnumun dibinde, BURNUMUN DİBİNDE!'

### 1140. `RD_ALifeLine_4f5378db58c7f21c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "It's breathing on me- no, it isn't, it ISN'T, oh God"
- **TR:** "Bana soluyor- hayır, solumuyor, SOLUMUYOR, Allah'ım"

### 1141. `RD_ALifeLine_55d21b8934a7e13a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Get it off the- get it away from me! AWAY!'
- **TR:** 'Çekin şunu- uzaklaştırın benden! UZAĞA!'

### 1142. `RD_ALifeLine_2ab144bd4fa6874d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Jesus, it's at arm's length, somebody help- HELP-"
- **TR:** 'Yarabbi, bir kol boyu ötede, biri yardım- YARDIM-'

### 1143. `RD_ALifeLine_14dc5b4868e7b1b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Nononono, it's gonna- it's gonna- somebody shoot it, SHOOT IT!"
- **TR:** 'Yokyokyok, şey yapacak- şey yapacak- biri vursun şunu, VURUN!'

### 1144. `RD_ALifeLine_253e8a6b4b1cf8b2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Get the hell AWAY from me, you rotten son of a bitch'
- **TR:** 'Defol git BENDEN, seni çürük orospu çocuğu'

### 1145. `RD_ALifeLine_3bb4d2991dcc55e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Not today, you dead bastard, not TODAY!'
- **TR:** 'Bugün olmaz, ölü piç, BUGÜN olmaz!'

### 1146. `RD_ALifeLine_47e526ee209663d1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "I'm going to bash your head in if you take one more step, hear me?!"
- **TR:** 'Bir adım daha atarsan kafanı ezeceğim, duydun mu?!'

### 1147. `RD_ALifeLine_20a75a95215de612` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Back off! Back OFF, goddamn you!'
- **TR:** "Geri çekil! GERİ ÇEKİL, Allah'ın belası!"

### 1148. `RD_ALifeLine_04826df01eb0c8f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Damn thing never gives up, does it? Never!'
- **TR:** 'Lanet şey hiç pes etmiyor, değil mi? Hiç!'

### 1149. `RD_ALifeLine_65f0ad106128ffbb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Right in my face again, huh...'
- **TR:** 'Yine tam suratımda, ha...'

### 1150. `RD_ALifeLine_3b3d6747657c6b68` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'There it is, right where it wants to be... right on me'
- **TR:** 'İşte orada, olmak istediği yerde... tam üstümde'

### 1151. `RD_ALifeLine_228f34c528b366dc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Close enough to smell it, close enough to know it's over for somebody"
- **TR:** 'Kokusunu alacak kadar yakın, birinin işinin bittiğini anlayacak kadar yakın'

### 1152. `RD_ALifeLine_74c903451c4d3f99` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "It's close, let me handle this"
- **TR:** 'Yakın, bu işi bana bırak'

### 1153. `RD_ALifeLine_029994e92c2d7e42` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Keep still, I'll take it"
- **TR:** 'Kıpırdama, ben hallederim'

### 1154. `RD_ALifeLine_5f857d5a5e562909` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "It's within reach, I've got it"
- **TR:** 'Uzanabileceğim yerde, bende'

### 1155. `RD_ALifeLine_7b1cf23e2a06a033` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Step aside, I'll finish it"
- **TR:** 'Çekil, ben bitiririm'

### 1156. `RD_ALifeLine_2497ebd311ea1496` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Easy, easy, I've got this one"
- **TR:** 'Sakin, sakin, bu bende'

### 1157. `RD_ALifeLine_581698c8281f8b8b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Oh, my Lord, it's too close, too close, honey, get behind me"
- **TR:** "Aman Allah'ım, çok yakın, çok yakın, yavrum, arkama geç"

### 1158. `RD_ALifeLine_25f5744f27b3ff1e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Don't you touch me, you hear? Don't you dare!"
- **TR:** 'Sakın bana dokunma, duydun mu? Sakın ha!'

### 1159. `RD_ALifeLine_3b60575f661b700c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Get back, you horrible thing, get BACK!'
- **TR:** 'Geri çekil, seni iğrenç şey, GERİ!'

### 1160. `RD_ALifeLine_0e1fba1d032da403` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Somebody help me, I can't run, I can't run!"
- **TR:** 'Biri yardım etsin, kaçamıyorum, kaçamıyorum!'

### 1161. `RD_ALifeLine_27b0969924183b45` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "I've lived too long to die like this, get AWAY!"
- **TR:** 'Böyle ölmek için fazla yaşadım, DEFOL!'

### 1162. `RD_ALifeLine_116dec0a04e95a70` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Oh, sweet Jesus, it's got a hand on me"
- **TR:** 'Yarabbi, elini üstüme koydu'

### 1163. `RD_ALifeLine_1b2d8de956c3bb0c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Dude! Dude, it's TOUCHING me, shit, get it OFF"
- **TR:** 'Lan! Lan, bana DOKUNUYOR, siktir, çekin ŞUNU'

### 1164. `RD_ALifeLine_4e8f0f4023ae9fa3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Not like this, not like this, I'm only seventeen!"
- **TR:** 'Böyle olmaz, böyle olmaz, daha on yedi yaşındayım!'

### 1165. `RD_ALifeLine_295dd5a75e596bed` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'AAHH! Mom! Dad! Somebody!'
- **TR:** 'AAHH! Anne! Baba! Biri!'

### 1166. `RD_ALifeLine_1f6f30002939e1bf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Holy shit, get back, get BACK, oh my God'
- **TR:** "Hassiktir, geri çekil, GERİ, aman Allah'ım"

### 1167. `RD_ALifeLine_0c93909b22793f8f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Fuck- sorry- fuck, it's on me, FUCK!"
- **TR:** 'Siktir- pardon- siktir, üstümde, SİKTİR!'

### 1168. `RD_ALifeLine_0466a5c4411fb91c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Get away from me! GET AWAY!'
- **TR:** 'Benden uzak dur! DEFOL!'

### 1169. `RD_ALifeLine_2a87ca26443564b7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Not me! I won't let you take me!"
- **TR:** 'Ben olmam! Beni almana izin vermeyeceğim!'

### 1170. `RD_ALifeLine_72797377640efd97` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Get your hands off her, get OFF her!'
- **TR:** 'Çek ellerini ondan, ÇEKİL üstünden!'

### 1171. `RD_ALifeLine_52d652462ccf55bf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Behind me, behind me, stay behind me, go go go!'
- **TR:** 'Arkama, arkama, arkamda kal, hadi hadi hadi!'

### 1172. `RD_ALifeLine_25f86f7f563f8514` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Back! Back! You're not touching my family!"
- **TR:** 'Geri! Geri! Aileme dokunamazsın!'

### 1173. `RD_ALifeLine_1c81c4306d7bc350` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "I'll beat you to death if I have to, come on, COME ON!"
- **TR:** 'Gerekirse seni döve döve öldürürüm, hadi, HADİ!'

### 1174. `RD_ALifeLine_668524ff71d295b6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Get behind me, all of you, now!'
- **TR:** 'Herkes arkama, hemen!'

### 1175. `RD_ALifeLine_6e8f9606334df111` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "It's here! Go, go, I've got it, I've got it!"
- **TR:** 'Burada! Gidin, gidin, bende, bende!'

### 1176. `RD_ALifeLine_6eec099d3c281516` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Oh Lord, protect us, it's upon us"
- **TR:** "Allah'ım, bizi koru, üstümüze geldi"

### 1177. `RD_ALifeLine_00a37b0d6ebb1ee5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Please, brother, I don't want to hurt you, but you're too close"
- **TR:** 'Lütfen kardeşim, canını yakmak istemiyorum ama çok yakınsın'

### 1178. `RD_ALifeLine_761eef572c2c6bce` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Forgive me, forgive me, forgive me, stay BACK'
- **TR:** 'Affet beni, affet beni, affet beni, GERİ DUR'

### 1179. `RD_ALifeLine_17e709db2bb1039d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Our Father who art- get back- who art in heaven- get BACK'
- **TR:** 'Göklerdeki Babamız- geri çekil- göklerdeki- GERİ ÇEKİL'

### 1180. `RD_ALifeLine_406a7b8c36c5f87e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Close! Close! Fall back and make a hole!'
- **TR:** 'Yakın! Yakın! Geri çekilin, yol açın!'

### 1181. `RD_ALifeLine_287f9f79615d5eac` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Hold it off me, hold it, I need space to swing'
- **TR:** 'Benden uzak tutun, tutun, savurmak için yer lazım'

### 1182. `RD_ALifeLine_3d63b7fa4d92b9fc` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Back up, back up, everybody back up, I've got it"
- **TR:** 'Geri, geri, herkes geri, bende'

### 1183. `RD_ALifeLine_67dfb71d4fa4e5da` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Tool in hand, I'm going to put this one down, step away"
- **TR:** 'Alet elimde, bunu indireceğim, uzaklaşın'

### 1184. `RD_ALifeLine_3ecf67a859c45c95` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Get away from my patient, get back from her NOW'
- **TR:** 'Hastamdan uzak dur, ondan HEMEN uzaklaş'

### 1185. `RD_ALifeLine_08c387c579bc4024` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "I can't treat that, I can't, step away from it, everyone"
- **TR:** 'Onu tedavi edemem, edemem, herkes ondan uzaklaşsın'

### 1186. `RD_ALifeLine_781a5b6139b17898` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Stay clear of its hands, everyone, stay clear, it's too close"
- **TR:** 'Herkes ellerinden uzak dursun, uzak durun, çok yakın'

### 1187. `RD_ALifeLine_35be86d72dc8a76e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Please, please don't make me do this"
- **TR:** 'Lütfen, lütfen bunu yapmak zorunda bırakma beni'

### 1188. `RD_ALifeLine_7125adcb7ba58381` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Aw hell, it's on top of us, step back, step back"
- **TR:** 'Hay ben böyle işin, tepemizde, geri çekil, geri çekil'

### 1189. `RD_ALifeLine_1d01692348d7ea28` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Watch out, I'll bust its head open if it comes another step"
- **TR:** 'Dikkat et, bir adım daha atarsa kafasını yararım'

### 1190. `RD_ALifeLine_1a5bdcc81ebc0635` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Hey! Hey! Get your hands off her, you ugly bastard'
- **TR:** 'Hey! Hey! Çek ellerini ondan, seni çirkin piç'

### 1191. `RD_ALifeLine_1571341423d7a4f9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Come on, then, come on, just you and me, you dead sucker'
- **TR:** 'Hadi o zaman, hadi, sen ve ben, seni ölü pislik'

### 1192. `RD_ALifeLine_569b8b5b3c72f57f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Get 'em off me, get 'em off!"
- **TR:** 'Çekin şunları üstümden, çekin!'

### 1193. `RD_ALifeLine_34bb32e270caf5eb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Too close, damn it, I got sloppy'
- **TR:** 'Çok yakın, kahretsin, dikkatsiz davrandım'

### 1194. `RD_ALifeLine_2ff2a1581a44b492` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "It slipped right up on me, how'd it do that"
- **TR:** 'Sezdirmeden yanıma sokuldu, bunu nasıl yaptı'

### 1195. `RD_ALifeLine_3267d1562aa1f364` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Stay away, I'm warning you, I'm warning you, stay away"
- **TR:** 'Uzak dur, uyarıyorum, uyarıyorum, uzak dur'

### 1196. `RD_ALifeLine_35491ddf44fc629f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'I can handle one, I can handle one, come on, then'
- **TR:** 'Bir taneyle başa çıkarım, bir taneyle başa çıkarım, hadi o zaman'

### 1197. `RD_ALifeLine_00c101e624c2759f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Don't touch me! Don't you touch me!"
- **TR:** 'Bana dokunma! Sakın bana dokunma!'

### 1198. `RD_ALifeLine_24254b3158c13003` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Back off or I'll swing this thing, I will!"
- **TR:** 'Geri çekil yoksa bunu savururum, savururum bak!'

### 1199. `RD_ALifeLine_638f39e14370bc5f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Oh God, oh God, oh God, it's right there"
- **TR:** "Aman Allah'ım, aman Allah'ım, aman Allah'ım, tam orada"

### 1200. `RD_ALifeLine_745636fa2b2a0ef1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Get back! GET BACK!'
- **TR:** 'Geri çekil! GERİ ÇEKİL!'

### 1201. `RD_ALifeLine_3508a10b4303015f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Come on, ya bastard, come on, I've got a bat for you"
- **TR:** 'Hadi, seni piç, hadi, sana bir sopam var'

### 1202. `RD_ALifeLine_37d1919301a03f04` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Right here, right here, I've got you, hold still"
- **TR:** 'Tam burada, tam burada, yakaladım seni, kıpırdama'

### 1203. `RD_ALifeLine_52d3d93b36a327f5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Back off, back off, you don't want any of this"
- **TR:** 'Geri çekil, geri çekil, bunu istemezsin'

### 1204. `RD_ALifeLine_41b7d99d062a98fb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Aw, shit, it's up on me, step back!"
- **TR:** 'Hay sıçayım, tepeme bindi, geri çekil!'

### 1205. `RD_ALifeLine_5dce368323f47169` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Don't let it touch you! Don't let it fucking touch you!"
- **TR:** 'Sana dokunmasına izin verme! Sakın o siktiğimin şeyi sana dokunmasın!'

### 1206. `RD_ALifeLine_0291902545076733` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'Its hands! Get its fucking hands away!'
- **TR:** 'Elleri! Uzaklaştır şu siktiğimin ellerini!'

### 1207. `RD_ALifeLine_742cfa642021f7c9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** 'The mouth! Watch its MOUTH!'
- **TR:** 'Ağzı! AĞZINA dikkat et!'

### 1208. `RD_ALifeLine_0f7dbe80587c724c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_zombie_1.lua, ZOMBIE_CLOSE*
- **EN:** "Don't get bitten! Please, please don't get bitten!"
- **TR:** 'Isırılma! Lütfen, lütfen ısırılma!'

### 1209. `RD_ALifeLine_3920389169438a28` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ow! Hey! Watch where you're swinging!"
- **TR:** 'Ah! Hey! Nereye savurduğuna dikkat et!'

### 1210. `RD_ALifeLine_1baf2dc86891dee4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Watch it! I'm not one of them!"
- **TR:** 'Dikkat et! Ben onlardan biri değilim!'

### 1211. `RD_ALifeLine_364580ee7f0ad456` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'OH YOU ARE SHOOTING AT ME! Stop! STOP!'
- **TR:** 'BANA ATEŞ EDİYORSUN! Dur! DUR!'

### 1212. `RD_ALifeLine_61928eb53a3c6d6b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'You shot me! You actually shot me! Why?!'
- **TR:** 'Beni vurdun! Gerçekten vurdun! Neden?!'

### 1213. `RD_ALifeLine_2a7424ad435a0f84` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'What are you doing? Put the gun down! Please!'
- **TR:** 'Ne yapıyorsun? İndir o silahı! Lütfen!'

### 1214. `RD_ALifeLine_73e7354a2ac16809` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Oh God, oh God, I'm bleeding, you shot me"
- **TR:** "Aman Allah'ım, aman Allah'ım, kanıyorum, beni vurdun"

### 1215. `RD_ALifeLine_56c07d3508f3f3df` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'You stabbed me! Why would you stab me?!'
- **TR:** 'Beni bıçakladın! Neden bıçaklarsın beni?!'

### 1216. `RD_ALifeLine_376888fa46002fcb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Ow! That knife, take it away from me!'
- **TR:** 'Ah! O bıçak, uzaklaştır onu benden!'

### 1217. `RD_ALifeLine_1cdb649515b2da0a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'You cut me! Why did you cut me?'
- **TR:** 'Beni kestin! Neden kestin beni?'

### 1218. `RD_ALifeLine_2cf1695f78bb4b76` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Ow! You hit me with a bat? What is wrong with you?'
- **TR:** 'Ah! Bana sopayla mı vurdun? Senin derdin ne?'

### 1219. `RD_ALifeLine_6fc99ec33b65007e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'My head! You hit me in the head with that pipe!'
- **TR:** 'Kafam! O boruyla kafama vurdun!'

### 1220. `RD_ALifeLine_54aeef764ab3b201` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "You bashed my arm! I think it's broken!"
- **TR:** 'Kolumu ezdin! Sanırım kırıldı!'

### 1221. `RD_ALifeLine_7d8b986c4f8120f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'You punched me! Why did you punch me?!'
- **TR:** 'Bana yumruk attın! Neden yumruk attın?!'

### 1222. `RD_ALifeLine_3a2ea6037eda49ea` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'You hit me with your car! Are you crazy?!'
- **TR:** 'Bana arabayla çarptın! Deli misin?!'

### 1223. `RD_ALifeLine_1f5f53cf59911528` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "You almost ran me over! Watch where you're driving!"
- **TR:** 'Az kalsın beni eziyordun! Nereye sürdüğüne bak!'

### 1224. `RD_ALifeLine_58c3f5f14856caeb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Your truck clipped me! My leg, my leg!'
- **TR:** 'Kamyonetin bana sürttü! Bacağım, bacağım!'

### 1225. `RD_ALifeLine_5c1e451877da9f3c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Hey! Watch that swing! Are you trying to kill me?'
- **TR:** 'Hey! Savurduğuna dikkat et! Beni öldürmeye mi çalışıyorsun?'

### 1226. `RD_ALifeLine_38f4effe63fdddc1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Whoa! Why would you swing at me? I didn't do anything!"
- **TR:** 'Oha! Neden bana savuruyorsun? Ben bir şey yapmadım!'

### 1227. `RD_ALifeLine_2c09375021408aca` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Whoa! Friendly! Friendly!'
- **TR:** 'Oha! Dostum! Dost!'

### 1228. `RD_ALifeLine_2be1c6e6761d35ad` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "What the hell? I'm on your side!"
- **TR:** 'Bu da ne be? Ben senin tarafındayım!'

### 1229. `RD_ALifeLine_10cf0cbe7cb3bbeb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ah! Why? Please, I didn't do anything!"
- **TR:** 'Ah! Neden? Lütfen, ben bir şey yapmadım!'

### 1230. `RD_ALifeLine_6dae1b0727d4dcd7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Oh God, you hurt me - please stop, please!'
- **TR:** "Aman Allah'ım, canımı yaktın - lütfen dur, lütfen!"

### 1231. `RD_ALifeLine_664713a55242788a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "No, no, I'm alive! I'm a person! Stop!"
- **TR:** 'Hayır, hayır, ben yaşıyorum! Ben insanım! Dur!'

### 1232. `RD_ALifeLine_50f0d4e44cd90415` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ow! Please, don't do this to me!"
- **TR:** 'Ah! Lütfen, bunu bana yapma!'

### 1233. `RD_ALifeLine_04c6b06a7f67d5f2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Ow! Please... I lost everybody already - just let me go, okay?'
- **TR:** 'Ah! Lütfen... zaten herkesi kaybettim - bırak gideyim, tamam mı?'

### 1234. `RD_ALifeLine_6f77ec693a9ce5b5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Stop! I'm not infected, look at me, I'm not!"
- **TR:** 'Dur! Enfekte değilim, bak bana, değilim!'

### 1235. `RD_ALifeLine_0e458c6a773e1924` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "It's okay, it was an accident, right? Right?"
- **TR:** 'Sorun yok, kazaydı, değil mi? Değil mi?'

### 1236. `RD_ALifeLine_15c4a28c653d5f4f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Ow, my shoulder! What did I ever do to you?'
- **TR:** 'Ah, omzum! Ben sana ne yaptım?'

### 1237. `RD_ALifeLine_2d00f1a63ca0d05e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ah! What's gotten into you?"
- **TR:** 'Ah! Sana ne oldu böyle?'

### 1238. `RD_ALifeLine_0c39499c72dfc789` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Hey! HEY! I said I was friendly, I said it twice!'
- **TR:** 'Hey! HEY! Dostum dedim, iki kere söyledim!'

### 1239. `RD_ALifeLine_5d45a8e56f49db75` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'What the hell was that for?'
- **TR:** 'Bu ne içindi be?'

### 1240. `RD_ALifeLine_486abaa30d6ca888` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ow! I'm not sick! Look at my eyes, I'm not sick!"
- **TR:** 'Ah! Hasta değilim! Gözlerime bak, hasta değilim!'

### 1241. `RD_ALifeLine_5558281f794c8b5c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Don't do it again. Please!"
- **TR:** 'Bir daha yapma. Lütfen!'

### 1242. `RD_ALifeLine_7f07099a4bac19f8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Please, I was walking away, I was already walking away'
- **TR:** 'Lütfen, uzaklaşıyordum, zaten uzaklaşıyordum'

### 1243. `RD_ALifeLine_22e51a302cc3dc7f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'I dropped the can, I dropped it - can I pick up the can?'
- **TR:** 'Konserveyi düşürdüm, düşürdüm - konserveyi alabilir miyim?'

### 1244. `RD_ALifeLine_173a58943bfd4ce6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ahh! Please! I don't want to fight!"
- **TR:** 'Ahh! Lütfen! Kavga etmek istemiyorum!'

### 1245. `RD_ALifeLine_2a7b03c76f9d2c6a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'You hit me with my hands full of laundry - laundry!'
- **TR:** 'Elim çamaşır doluyken vurdun bana - çamaşır!'

### 1246. `RD_ALifeLine_23bdc013123cfcd5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Ow! What is wrong with you?!'
- **TR:** 'Ah! Senin derdin ne?!'

### 1247. `RD_ALifeLine_2f8239804baf7d37` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "You can't just hit people! That's not-- you can't do that!"
- **TR:** 'İnsanlara öyle vuramazsın! Bu- bunu yapamazsın!'

### 1248. `RD_ALifeLine_203ee9d628385198` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ow! I'm seventy-one years old, you animal!"
- **TR:** 'Ah! Yetmiş bir yaşındayım, seni hayvan!'

### 1249. `RD_ALifeLine_459c5ead00484181` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Damn you! That how you treat everybody?'
- **TR:** 'Allah belanı versin! Herkese böyle mi davranırsın?'

### 1250. `RD_ALifeLine_7ba4b9d132347240` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Son of a... Watch your fire!'
- **TR:** 'Hay senin... Atışına dikkat et!'

### 1251. `RD_ALifeLine_19fe4fbf786a339e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Hey! Check your target before you pull!'
- **TR:** 'Hey! Tetiği çekmeden önce hedefine bak!'

### 1252. `RD_ALifeLine_4281b7a82be3597f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'I told you to keep back! Now look what you did!'
- **TR:** 'Uzak dur demiştim! Bak şimdi ne yaptın!'

### 1253. `RD_ALifeLine_56fb3e7550c61ef7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Real smart, now I'm hurt and annoyed"
- **TR:** 'Çok akıllıca, şimdi hem yaralıyım hem sinirli'

### 1254. `RD_ALifeLine_7994accd34dff3d3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Well, that's just rude"
- **TR:** 'Pek kaba oldu bu'

### 1255. `RD_ALifeLine_16c56d362f710186` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ah! That's my arm, I need that arm!"
- **TR:** 'Ah! Bu benim kolum, o kola ihtiyacım var!'

### 1256. `RD_ALifeLine_337a2a1a5a3c3895` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Ah, hell! You looking for a fight?'
- **TR:** 'Hay ben böyle işin! Kavga mı arıyorsun?'

### 1257. `RD_ALifeLine_6907ab5e4c0e05ff` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Hey! I fix things! Fixing things is the whole reason to keep me around!'
- **TR:** 'Hey! Ben tamirciyim! Beni yanında tutmanın tek sebebi bu!'

### 1258. `RD_ALifeLine_7a7e0b5e7ba0b55f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "You want to go? I've got a pipe wrench and a bad attitude"
- **TR:** 'Kapışmak mı istiyorsun? Bende boru anahtarı da var, kötü huy da'

### 1259. `RD_ALifeLine_5c50731611badb18` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ow! I'm the medic, idiot! Who's gonna patch me up now?"
- **TR:** 'Ah! Sağlıkçı benim, salak! Şimdi beni kim yamayacak?'

### 1260. `RD_ALifeLine_4b643c1320fcfbfe` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ow! I'm the one who stitches people! Me! This hand!"
- **TR:** 'Ah! İnsanları diken benim! Ben! Bu el!'

### 1261. `RD_ALifeLine_363ceecf787b72e4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "That's a laceration, I know, because I'm looking at my own arm"
- **TR:** 'Bu bir kesik yarası, biliyorum, çünkü kendi koluma bakıyorum'

### 1262. `RD_ALifeLine_5a2159c10aef066f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Lord give me patience - stop that!'
- **TR:** "Allah'ım sabır ver - kes şunu!"

### 1263. `RD_ALifeLine_13dabf114eb9ee1e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Hey! Easy! Fire department!'
- **TR:** 'Hey! Sakin! İtfaiye!'

### 1264. `RD_ALifeLine_77a2ef8c051d751e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'You hit a fireman, in Knox County - in July - think that through'
- **TR:** "Knox County'de bir itfaiyeciye vurdun - hem de temmuzda - bunu bir düşün"

### 1265. `RD_ALifeLine_0f188c6d6953d671` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Ow, my bad shoulder, you went for the bad one, damn'
- **TR:** 'Ah, sakat omzum, tam sakat olana vurdun, kahretsin'

### 1266. `RD_ALifeLine_61af520e2a5c8774` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'You hurt me like it was nothing at all'
- **TR:** 'Hiçbir şey değilmiş gibi canımı yaktın'

### 1267. `RD_ALifeLine_498e8cdc0e119d62` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Ah! Get back, get back, leave me alone!'
- **TR:** 'Ah! Geri çekil, geri çekil, beni rahat bırak!'

### 1268. `RD_ALifeLine_7dbef440238e1cbf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ow! I haven't done a thing to you!"
- **TR:** 'Ah! Ben sana hiçbir şey yapmadım!'

### 1269. `RD_ALifeLine_3e4fd7d423e3a6bd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ow! You did that on purpose, didn't you?"
- **TR:** 'Ah! Bunu bilerek yaptın, değil mi?'

### 1270. `RD_ALifeLine_07d1d01145e897bd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'What the hell are you doing? Leave me be!'
- **TR:** 'Ne halt ediyorsun sen? Rahat bırak beni!'

### 1271. `RD_ALifeLine_3a9314c30b136880` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Don't touch me again. I mean that"
- **TR:** 'Bana bir daha dokunma. Ciddiyim'

### 1272. `RD_ALifeLine_15d691fc55653b66` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Son of a-- I've took worse off a drill press - don't push it"
- **TR:** 'Hay senin-- matkaptan bundan beterini yedim - üstüme gelme'

### 1273. `RD_ALifeLine_718509270aea04a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'I got enough to put up with. Quit it!'
- **TR:** 'Zaten çekeceğim yeterince dert var. Kes şunu!'

### 1274. `RD_ALifeLine_6ad700886452395e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Easy! You're going to get somebody killed!"
- **TR:** 'Sakin! Birinin ölmesine sebep olacaksın!'

### 1275. `RD_ALifeLine_6ec55a682dc359c6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ah! Easy! I'm holding water, look, just water!"
- **TR:** 'Ah! Sakin! Elimde su var, bak, sadece su!'

### 1276. `RD_ALifeLine_3e14056167354a56` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Hey! Wrong one! I'm the one who waved!"
- **TR:** 'Hey! Yanlış kişi! El sallayan bendim!'

### 1277. `RD_ALifeLine_10c92ac059bc9ce3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Whoa, whoa! We were talking! We were just talking!'
- **TR:** 'Oha, oha! Konuşuyorduk! Sadece konuşuyorduk!'

### 1278. `RD_ALifeLine_4513eba4479f97e1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'I told you to keep back from the fence, you kept coming'
- **TR:** 'Çitten uzak dur dedim, gelmeye devam ettin'

### 1279. `RD_ALifeLine_3e0546b204f99439` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'What do you want from me? Tell me!'
- **TR:** 'Benden ne istiyorsun? Söyle!'

### 1280. `RD_ALifeLine_7db841e665f9fd6b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Why would you do that to me?'
- **TR:** 'Bunu bana neden yaptın?'

### 1281. `RD_ALifeLine_220fdea615e45610` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Stop! I'm a person, damn it!"
- **TR:** 'Dur! Ben insanım, kahretsin!'

### 1282. `RD_ALifeLine_3ec431e40fa21021` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Hey! You don't get to do that!"
- **TR:** 'Hey! Bunu yapmaya hakkın yok!'

### 1283. `RD_ALifeLine_468a9de32b81ef17` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Don't hurt me just because you can!"
- **TR:** 'Sırf yapabiliyorsun diye canımı yakma!'

### 1284. `RD_ALifeLine_403774b45f4a36e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Back away from me. Right now'
- **TR:** 'Benden uzaklaş. Hemen'

### 1285. `RD_ALifeLine_6fb981331445fdf3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "I didn't ask for this!"
- **TR:** 'Ben bunu istemedim!'

### 1286. `RD_ALifeLine_1213025a33aef218` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Ow! Keep your hands to yourself!'
- **TR:** 'Ah! Elini kendine sakla!'

### 1287. `RD_ALifeLine_2c3fccd3337b52bd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'You could have just talked to me!'
- **TR:** 'Benimle konuşabilirdin!'

### 1288. `RD_ALifeLine_4618f96a2df68a2d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Enough. Don't try that again"
- **TR:** 'Yeter. Bir daha deneme'

### 1289. `RD_ALifeLine_1b09f36f2b3a7a99` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Is this really what you've come to?"
- **TR:** 'Sen gerçekten bu hâle mi geldin?'

### 1290. `RD_ALifeLine_7e6324173138dcf5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Don't make me fight you!"
- **TR:** 'Seninle dövüşmek zorunda bırakma beni!'

### 1291. `RD_ALifeLine_51d688c475435e6e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'That hurt, you asshole!'
- **TR:** 'Canım yandı, seni şerefsiz!'

### 1292. `RD_ALifeLine_00e572150cebb7f5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'I felt that! What is wrong with you?'
- **TR:** 'Hissettim onu! Senin derdin ne?'

### 1293. `RD_ALifeLine_4dd2378c7b971f14` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Stabbing people now? You've lost your mind"
- **TR:** 'Artık insanları mı bıçaklıyorsun? Aklını kaçırmışsın'

### 1294. `RD_ALifeLine_4e34310c484bc41d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'A bat, really? Get away from me!'
- **TR:** 'Sopa mı, cidden? Uzak dur benden!'

### 1295. `RD_ALifeLine_38be51026cc30d58` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'You hit me in the face! Get away from me!'
- **TR:** 'Suratıma vurdun! Uzak dur benden!'

### 1296. `RD_ALifeLine_5576cd1a4d2daa3f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "I KNEW it, I knew you'd do something, everybody said, everybody said!"
- **TR:** 'BİLİYORDUM, bir şey yapacağını biliyordum, herkes söyledi, herkes söyledi!'

### 1297. `RD_ALifeLine_11ea8fbf1afb5da0` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Oh God, it's true, all of it, the stories are true!"
- **TR:** "Aman Allah'ım, doğruymuş, hepsi, hikâyeler doğruymuş!"

### 1298. `RD_ALifeLine_63c01338147b7d38` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Please, please, no more, no more, I'll give you everything!"
- **TR:** 'Lütfen, lütfen, yeter, yeter, her şeyimi veririm!'

### 1299. `RD_ALifeLine_1f018538476c1ac8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Just like they said you would!! Figures, it figures!'
- **TR:** 'Tam da dedikleri gibi!! Belliydi, belliydi zaten!'

### 1300. `RD_ALifeLine_1086225c3c24754c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Ow!! You bastard, I knew I couldn't trust you!"
- **TR:** 'Ah!! Seni piç, sana güvenemeyeceğimi biliyordum!'

### 1301. `RD_ALifeLine_14c7c6ad755a5e97` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "Why?! After everything I heard, I should've known!"
- **TR:** 'Neden?! Onca şey duyduktan sonra bilmem gerekirdi!'

### 1302. `RD_ALifeLine_4e202e9c69623338` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** "What?! Why?! You're supposed to be the good one!"
- **TR:** 'Ne?! Neden?! İyi olan sen değil miydin?!'

### 1303. `RD_ALifeLine_79479d2d12f2d60e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Ow!! What was that for?! I thought you were different!'
- **TR:** 'Ah!! Bu ne içindi?! Sen farklısın sanıyordum!'

### 1304. `RD_ALifeLine_29a9cdcc23df6678` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Oh my God, not you, not you, why?!'
- **TR:** "Aman Allah'ım, sen olamazsın, sen olamazsın, neden?!"

### 1305. `RD_ALifeLine_1951602d0dab6f7d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER*
- **EN:** 'Not the Legend... what did I do?! What did I even DO?!'
- **TR:** 'Efsane değil... ben ne yaptım?! Ne YAPTIM ki?!'

### 1306. `RD_ALifeLine_40ffa27b267a80c6` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Stop! Please! I'll give you anything, just stop hitting me!"
- **TR:** 'Dur! Lütfen! Ne istersen veririm, yeter ki vurmayı kes!'

### 1307. `RD_ALifeLine_585970ea03736418` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Why are you doing this? I'm begging you!"
- **TR:** 'Bunu neden yapıyorsun? Yalvarıyorum!'

### 1308. `RD_ALifeLine_323a3aa904e469af` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Please- PLEASE, take it, take whatever you want!'
- **TR:** 'Lütfen- LÜTFEN, al, ne istiyorsan al!'

### 1309. `RD_ALifeLine_37652f423e12c6ba` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Not again, please, not again!'
- **TR:** 'Yine olmaz, lütfen, yine olmaz!'

### 1310. `RD_ALifeLine_5056b11541fcd9f4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I can't take another one - please - please"
- **TR:** 'Bir tane daha kaldıramam - lütfen - lütfen'

### 1311. `RD_ALifeLine_000637bb200253ff` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "That wasn't an accident, oh God, that wasn't an accident"
- **TR:** "O kaza değildi, aman Allah'ım, o kaza değildi"

### 1312. `RD_ALifeLine_15e29b854ae52423` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Please, I'm on the ground, I'm on the ground! Stop!"
- **TR:** 'Lütfen, yerdeyim, yerdeyim! Dur!'

### 1313. `RD_ALifeLine_6c76439e7ab9884d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "You're enjoying this, oh God, you're enjoying this"
- **TR:** "Bundan zevk alıyorsun, aman Allah'ım, zevk alıyorsun"

### 1314. `RD_ALifeLine_0d9da9127d047863` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "My family needs me! I'm all they've got!"
- **TR:** 'Ailemin bana ihtiyacı var! Benden başka kimseleri yok!'

### 1315. `RD_ALifeLine_3776100744448ffa` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Mom! MOM! Somebody help me!'
- **TR:** 'Anne! ANNE! Biri yardım etsin!'

### 1316. `RD_ALifeLine_4b4851b7779d5f3f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Mama said run! Mama said run, so I'm running!"
- **TR:** 'Annem kaç dedi! Annem kaç dedi, kaçıyorum!'

### 1317. `RD_ALifeLine_5bc286fc1b6763e2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Lord have mercy, you mean it, you really mean it'
- **TR:** "Allah'ım sen merhamet et, ciddisin, gerçekten ciddisin"

### 1318. `RD_ALifeLine_685dd2be1b571887` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Two times, I'm eighty-one years old and you hit me two times"
- **TR:** 'İki kere, seksen bir yaşındayım ve bana iki kere vurdun'

### 1319. `RD_ALifeLine_4d6ae0db2fc8bbdb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Go on then, I buried a husband and two brothers, you don't scare me"
- **TR:** 'Hadi o zaman, bir koca ve iki kardeş gömdüm, beni korkutamazsın'

### 1320. `RD_ALifeLine_08c047ea10af4467` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Now you've done it - get the shotgun, Earl!"
- **TR:** 'Bak şimdi yaptın yapacağını - tüfeği getir, Earl!'

### 1321. `RD_ALifeLine_24854076636abd93` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Twice? Twice is not a mistake - back off!'
- **TR:** 'İki kere mi? İki kere kaza olmaz - geri çekil!'

### 1322. `RD_ALifeLine_511f9f510c9531b9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Alright, you've made your choice!"
- **TR:** 'Pekâlâ, seçimini yaptın!'

### 1323. `RD_ALifeLine_59d4ef064dd0935c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Do that one more time and I'll put you down"
- **TR:** 'Bunu bir kere daha yaparsan seni indiririm'

### 1324. `RD_ALifeLine_660ba3683756540e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Again? Oh, you rotten piece of work!'
- **TR:** 'Yine mi? Ah, seni çürük herif!'

### 1325. `RD_ALifeLine_44eab4a74232bc23` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'I warned you, I warned you twice!'
- **TR:** 'Uyarmıştım, iki kere uyarmıştım!'

### 1326. `RD_ALifeLine_1ab406165446b49e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Twice, you're lucky I'm this tired"
- **TR:** 'İki kere, bu kadar yorgun olduğuma şükret'

### 1327. `RD_ALifeLine_0f4293df46cb056f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Twice, now I've got to care about you, I hate that"
- **TR:** 'İki kere, şimdi seni umursamak zorundayım, nefret ediyorum bundan'

### 1328. `RD_ALifeLine_257b813163fd4ba1` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Okay, now I'm mad, now I'm real mad!"
- **TR:** 'Tamam, şimdi sinirlendim, şimdi fena sinirlendim!'

### 1329. `RD_ALifeLine_18ddf297161f3905` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Right, I've had enough of you - come on, then"
- **TR:** 'Peki, yeter artık senden - hadi o zaman'

### 1330. `RD_ALifeLine_00db5e327888d44c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Twice! Hit me again and I'll break your damn arm"
- **TR:** 'İki kere! Bir daha vurursan o lanet kolunu kırarım'

### 1331. `RD_ALifeLine_2f4a523c3d75e2a7` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Fine, wrench it is'
- **TR:** 'Peki, İngiliz anahtarı konuşsun o zaman'

### 1332. `RD_ALifeLine_69bac50379d93dfb` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'You hit a man holding a hammer - twice - think on that a second'
- **TR:** 'Elinde çekiç olan bir adama vurdun - hem de iki kere - bunu bir saniye düşün'

### 1333. `RD_ALifeLine_6edca43153782100` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'I stitch people up for a living, I know how to do the opposite'
- **TR:** 'Ekmeğimi insanları dikerek kazanıyorum, tersini yapmayı da bilirim'

### 1334. `RD_ALifeLine_5a0d47835671277e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I've patched men who did less than you - get back off me"
- **TR:** 'Senden daha azını yapan adamları yamadım - çekil üstümden'

### 1335. `RD_ALifeLine_53428b6e5cef3d57` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Hit me again and see who stitches you up next time'
- **TR:** 'Bir daha vur da bak bakalım seni sonra kim dikecek'

### 1336. `RD_ALifeLine_46755fe854777949` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I turned the other cheek, I'm fresh out of cheeks!"
- **TR:** 'Öbür yanağımı da çevirdim, çevirecek yanağım kalmadı!'

### 1337. `RD_ALifeLine_473b6a457464be0c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "The Lord's done talking, so am I"
- **TR:** "Rab'bin sözü bitti, benimki de"

### 1338. `RD_ALifeLine_4a135cff69a8a3a5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I'll pray for you after - get back"
- **TR:** 'Sonra senin için dua ederim - geri çekil'

### 1339. `RD_ALifeLine_35605a121ccf0e48` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "That's it! I'm done being nice!"
- **TR:** 'Bu kadar! İyi davranmayı bıraktım!'

### 1340. `RD_ALifeLine_308154e00fe9eec5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Again?! I didn't do anything! I didn't do ANYTHING!"
- **TR:** 'Yine mi?! Ben bir şey yapmadım! Hiçbir şey YAPMADIM!'

### 1341. `RD_ALifeLine_1e037f321dc50ac9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "You're going to kill me, you're actually going to kill me"
- **TR:** 'Beni öldüreceksin, gerçekten beni öldüreceksin'

### 1342. `RD_ALifeLine_5c2c49a707c44202` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Stop! I'll give you the bag, I'll give you my shoes, stop!"
- **TR:** 'Dur! Çantayı veririm, ayakkabılarımı veririm, dur!'

### 1343. `RD_ALifeLine_60476efb4e80d369` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Oh God, you really mean it, stop- please stop!'
- **TR:** "Aman Allah'ım, gerçekten ciddisin, dur- lütfen dur!"

### 1344. `RD_ALifeLine_3a6bf172190d8a0e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Why are you smiling? Don't you smile at me!"
- **TR:** 'Neden gülümsüyorsun? Bana gülümseme!'

### 1345. `RD_ALifeLine_24fc92e51535d9b5` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'I begged you, I begged, and you did it again'
- **TR:** 'Yalvardım sana, yalvardım, sen yine yaptın'

### 1346. `RD_ALifeLine_4c13619a17146f1a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I can't run, my knee went out on the fourth - so do what you're doing"
- **TR:** 'Kaçamam, ayın dördünde dizim çıktı - o yüzden ne yapıyorsan yap'

### 1347. `RD_ALifeLine_6c3f2d5e766e6a17` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I ain't gonna fight you, I'm just gonna stand here and bleed at you"
- **TR:** 'Seninle dövüşmeyeceğim, burada dikilip karşında kan kaybedeceğim'

### 1348. `RD_ALifeLine_7ea15a0a502a2ff3` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "You hit me before and you're doing it again!"
- **TR:** 'Daha önce de vurdun, şimdi yine vuruyorsun!'

### 1349. `RD_ALifeLine_63934e7125d90b66` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "My name's Wanda - I'm Wanda - does that do anything at all?"
- **TR:** "Benim adım Wanda - ben Wanda'yım - bu bir şey ifade ediyor mu?"

### 1350. `RD_ALifeLine_2d3a81547fff34ab` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "That's twice! Keep away from me!"
- **TR:** 'İki oldu! Uzak dur benden!'

### 1351. `RD_ALifeLine_4ca8056f2db13d6e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Earl! EARL! Get the twelve gauge!'
- **TR:** 'Earl! EARL! Tüfeği getir!'

### 1352. `RD_ALifeLine_21ff8cde5036983c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Twice! I'll remember your face, you hear me?"
- **TR:** 'İki kere! Yüzünü unutmayacağım, duydun mu?'

### 1353. `RD_ALifeLine_0583f8527c7779d2` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "You're a grown person doing this, a grown person"
- **TR:** 'Koca insansın, bunu yapıyorsun, koca insan'

### 1354. `RD_ALifeLine_242155901d0e9bf8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "You're between me and my family, nothing else matters now"
- **TR:** 'Benimle ailemin arasındasın, artık başka hiçbir şeyin önemi yok'

### 1355. `RD_ALifeLine_01558ad073a2c474` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I've got a knife in this apron and I've dressed hogs with it"
- **TR:** 'Önlüğümde bir bıçak var, onunla domuz yüzdüm'

### 1356. `RD_ALifeLine_3a1221cb0d9e217b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'You picked the wrong shift to try that on'
- **TR:** 'Bunu denemek için yanlış vardiyayı seçtin'

### 1357. `RD_ALifeLine_2f5ed9e326743f44` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I've been on a roof in August, you don't frighten me any"
- **TR:** 'Ağustosta çatıda çalıştım, beni hiç korkutamazsın'

### 1358. `RD_ALifeLine_3d57d04a3457ee70` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "That's it, no bandages for your kind - not ever"
- **TR:** 'Bu kadar, senin gibilere sargı bezi yok - asla'

### 1359. `RD_ALifeLine_60b7a43f2ada04bf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I've carried men bigger than you down stairs - come on"
- **TR:** 'Senden iri adamları merdivenden aşağı taşıdım - hadi gel'

### 1360. `RD_ALifeLine_451867a65a32bac4` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'I warned you already. You chose not to listen'
- **TR:** 'Seni zaten uyardım. Dinlememeyi seçtin'

### 1361. `RD_ALifeLine_3838dbdd6d34814c` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'I said stop! Did you hear me?'
- **TR:** 'Dur dedim! Duymadın mı?'

### 1362. `RD_ALifeLine_4d051aef5d8881b8` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "You're doing this on purpose!"
- **TR:** 'Bunu bilerek yapıyorsun!'

### 1363. `RD_ALifeLine_499fecfe5ada994f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Again? You want me dead that badly?'
- **TR:** 'Yine mi? Ölmemi o kadar mı istiyorsun?'

### 1364. `RD_ALifeLine_46494b8d7bf84519` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Get away! I mean it this time!'
- **TR:** 'Defol! Bu sefer ciddiyim!'

### 1365. `RD_ALifeLine_2a09b0d81f443d1b` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "You don't know when to leave off!"
- **TR:** 'Ne zaman duracağını bilmiyorsun!'

### 1366. `RD_ALifeLine_4688702b1715da1a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I'm not standing here for another one!"
- **TR:** 'Bir tane daha yemek için burada durmayacağım!'

### 1367. `RD_ALifeLine_4bf816126fef94dd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "That's enough out of you!"
- **TR:** 'Bu kadarı yeter!'

### 1368. `RD_ALifeLine_04cf9c13145979bf` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Stop coming at me!'
- **TR:** 'Üstüme gelmeyi kes!'

### 1369. `RD_ALifeLine_202a4c7329c7d230` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'I gave you a chance to quit!'
- **TR:** 'Bırakman için sana fırsat verdim!'

### 1370. `RD_ALifeLine_4ec0a0f14afd7696` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'You keep doing it! Why?'
- **TR:** 'Yapmaya devam ediyorsun! Neden?'

### 1371. `RD_ALifeLine_5ff0570e54ee8a94` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'How many times do I have to tell you?'
- **TR:** 'Sana kaç kere söylemem lazım?'

### 1372. `RD_ALifeLine_0645701b3ec60b44` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Not again! Keep away!'
- **TR:** 'Yine olmaz! Uzak dur!'

### 1373. `RD_ALifeLine_1de82384352b8501` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "You're really going to keep hurting me?"
- **TR:** 'Gerçekten canımı yakmaya devam mı edeceksin?'

### 1374. `RD_ALifeLine_5410bd876204398d` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I know it wasn't an accident now!"
- **TR:** 'Artık kaza olmadığını biliyorum!'

### 1375. `RD_ALifeLine_6b79800110561f42` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'You shot me again! Why are you doing this?'
- **TR:** 'Beni yine vurdun! Bunu neden yapıyorsun?'

### 1376. `RD_ALifeLine_6197ea7f64ca838e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Stop shooting at me! Please! I didn't do anything!"
- **TR:** 'Bana ateş etmeyi kes! Lütfen! Ben bir şey yapmadım!'

### 1377. `RD_ALifeLine_1c3d5673108da23e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Not again! Another shot! You are trying to kill me!'
- **TR:** 'Yine olmaz! Bir atış daha! Beni öldürmeye çalışıyorsun!'

### 1378. `RD_ALifeLine_65be8eeb49376603` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'You stabbed me AGAIN?! What did I do to you!'
- **TR:** 'Beni YİNE mi bıçakladın?! Ben sana ne yaptım!'

### 1379. `RD_ALifeLine_7b2160fe1463a542` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Stop slashing at me! I'll give you whatever you want!"
- **TR:** 'Bıçak savurmayı kes! Ne istersen veririm!'

### 1380. `RD_ALifeLine_66e687d810ceceb9` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'You hit me again! With the bat! Stop, please!'
- **TR:** 'Yine vurdun! Sopayla! Dur, lütfen!'

### 1381. `RD_ALifeLine_28f1074226a31882` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Not the pipe again! Please! I'm bleeding!"
- **TR:** 'Yine boruyla olmaz! Lütfen! Kanıyorum!'

### 1382. `RD_ALifeLine_6681eebc5cf793ec` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Another punch! Please stop, I'll go away!"
- **TR:** 'Bir yumruk daha! Lütfen dur, giderim!'

### 1383. `RD_ALifeLine_6f2c827847510cda` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Stop hitting me in the face! Why are you doing this?'
- **TR:** 'Suratıma vurmayı kes! Bunu neden yapıyorsun?'

### 1384. `RD_ALifeLine_4f9fa44d1db34f82` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Stop swinging at me! Stop!'
- **TR:** 'Bana savurmayı kes! Dur!'

### 1385. `RD_ALifeLine_214679015fd4ff10` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'You hit me with that truck again! Stop it!'
- **TR:** 'Yine o kamyonetle çarptın! Kes şunu!'

### 1386. `RD_ALifeLine_4b82566f1d49546e` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "I knew it!! I knew you'd do this, please, please stop, I'll give you whatever you want!"
- **TR:** 'Biliyordum!! Bunu yapacağını biliyordum, lütfen, lütfen dur, ne istersen veririm!'

### 1387. `RD_ALifeLine_08792a183c12026f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "God no, no more, no more, I can't take it, please!"
- **TR:** "Allah'ım, yeter, yeter, dayanamıyorum, lütfen!"

### 1388. `RD_ALifeLine_3f2c651f0c00c83a` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Again?! What the hell is wrong with you?!'
- **TR:** 'Yine mi?! Senin derdin ne be?!'

### 1389. `RD_ALifeLine_10695302348a7196` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Stop it!! Stop hitting me, you coward!'
- **TR:** 'Kes şunu!! Vurmayı kes, seni korkak!'

### 1390. `RD_ALifeLine_336e42207241f2dd` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** 'Why?! Why are you doing this?! I thought you were different!'
- **TR:** 'Neden?! Bunu neden yapıyorsun?! Sen farklısın sanıyordum!'

### 1391. `RD_ALifeLine_34c500e200fc2f1f` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Please stop, please, I don't understand, what did I do?"
- **TR:** 'Lütfen dur, lütfen, anlamıyorum, ben ne yaptım?'

### 1392. `RD_ALifeLine_187d05da7d95e523` — Claude (yeni)
*Bağlam: kod=ALifeDialogue_barks_hit_1.lua, HIT_BY_PLAYER_AGAIN*
- **EN:** "Not you, not you, you're supposed to be the good one, please, why?"
- **TR:** 'Sen olamazsın, sen olamazsın, iyi olan sen değil miydin, lütfen, neden?'
